Abstract: Benchmarks for LLM-agent memory typically generate conversations first and extract answer keys afterwards -- with documented label-error and contamination problems -- and they overwhelmingly measure short interaction histories. We invert the pipeline: a seeded life-script sampler emits facts with validity intervals, volatility classes, and source channels before any text exists; an LLM renderer writes chat and email from per-event fact manifests; a fidelity verifier confirms every planted fact; and questions are instantiated mechanically from the script, so gold answers are script-valid by construction and separately validated for answerability. The synthetic, fictionalized corpus (~380 questions, 15 types) embeds features absent from the benchmarks we survey: per-fact validity intervals, sent/received trust distinctions, injection probes in a benign harness, and as-of-date question sets. Benchmarking five memory architectures against a no-memory control (fixed answerer, versioned LLM judge, three replicates, two horizons), we find backend rankings invert with history length: the budgeted curated-map memory that leads at three weeks loses recall of evicted content by nine weeks (96% to 72%) while a provenance-typed graph rises to 90%; the inversion is positive for all six users under complete cross-family re-judging (exact p=0.031). A full-rendered-history baseline ties or exceeds the best memory system at the short horizon but shows no judge-independent advantage at nine weeks, at about twice the read cost. Write-stage quality strongly correlates with downstream quality (weakly-written facts fail 24% vs 2%), and injection resistance tracked whether provenance boundaries survive representation. A layered architecture performs best among the memory systems in both regimes (96.8% short-horizon) and is released as Veracium, an open-source library, with the corpus generator and harness.

</details>


### [67] [MoE$^2$-LoRA: When MoE Models Meet MoE-style Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21978)
*Qingyu Yang,Haonan He,Minglei Li,Jingqi Ye,Tao Chen,Lei Bai,Peng Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MoE²-LoRA方法，通过双通道路由条件投影模块和全局LoRA专家池，首次实现MoE风格的低秩适应微调，在下游任务上达到SOTA精度并保持泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型的参数高效微调方法要么使用统一适配器忽略路由先验导致效率低和遗忘风险，要么依赖静态专家选择限制每token容量和跨专家特征学习，缺乏对MoE模型特化的高效微调方案。

Method: 提出MoE²-LoRA，包含两个核心设计：(1) 双通道路由条件投影(RCP)模块，复用基础路由器激活值指导LoRA路由，深度耦合预训练专家特化与任务自适应；(2) 全局LoRA专家池，跨所有层共享，实现模型级自适应并产生涌现的层间亲和性和均衡的专家利用。

Result: 在多种不同规模和专家粒度的MoE骨干模型上评估，MoE²-LoRA一致达到下游任务SOTA准确率，同时保持更强的通用能力。

Conclusion: MoE²-LoRA同时具备先验复用、动态适配器路由和模型级知识共享的优势，为MoE模型的高效微调提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have been widely adopted in large language models, yet parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) for MoE models remains underexplored. Existing PEFT methods for MoE either ignore router priors with uniform adapters, reducing efficiency and risking forgetting, or rely on static expert selection, limiting per-token capacity and cross-expert feature learning. In this paper, we make the first attempt to fine-tune MoE models with MoE-style low-rank adaptation: our method, entitled MoE$^2$-LoRA, deeply couples the pretrained expert specialization with task-specific adaptivity via a dual-channel Routing-Conditioned Projection (RCP) module, which reuses base router activations to inform LoRA routing. We further introduce a single global LoRA expert pool shared across all layers, enabling model-wide adaptation with emergent layer-wise affinities and balanced expert utilization. MoE$^2$-LoRA simultaneously benefits from the advantages of prior reuse, dynamic adapter routing, and model-wide knowledge sharing. Evaluated on multiple MoE backbones with varying scales and expert granularities, MoE$^2$-LoRA consistently achieves state-of-the-art downstream accuracy while retaining stronger general capabilities.

</details>


### [68] [J-CoT: Chain-of-Thought in J-Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21981)
*Junde Wu,Jiayuan Zhu,Fengling Liu,Minhao Hu,Jiazhen Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出J-CoT，一种基于词汇索引坐标系（J-space）的循环推理框架。它在每个推理周期用完整隐空间计算，周期边界则将中间状态表示为词汇索引系数（J-thought），而非完整的隐状态或自然语言文本。实验表明，J-CoT-Zero在同等条件下匹配或超越最强潜在推理基线，J-CoT-Train在数学、科学、编程和结构化路径推理任务上取得最高分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Chain-of-thought方法强制中间状态必须用自然语言表达，但许多瞬态计算无需完全言语化，这限制了推理效率。另一方面，现有潜在推理方法虽可传播连续隐状态，但缺乏选择和组织信息的显式机制。因此，需要一个既保持语言基础又无需解码完整句子的中间接口。

Method: 基于J-space（词汇索引坐标系），在每个推理周期内模型在完整隐空间计算，周期边界处将中间状态映射为词汇索引系数（J-thought），将其前向传递，再映射回下一周期的隐表示。J-thought既非完整自然语言也非稠密隐状态，而是词汇索引上的稀疏系数。

Result: 在匹配的骨干网络和推理设置下，J-CoT-Zero在所有基准上匹配或超越最强潜在推理基线；J-CoT-Train在数学、科学、编程和结构化路径推理任务上取得最高分。

Conclusion: J-CoT通过在言语化和纯隐状态之间引入词汇索引系数作为中间接口，有效结合了两类方法的优势，既无需完整自然语言中间步骤，又提供了显式的信息选择机制，显著提升了推理性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought prompting improves language-model reasoning by carrying intermediate states across successive computation steps. However, relying on natural language as the only recurrent interface is overly restrictive, since many transient computations do not need to be fully verbalized. Existing latent-reasoning methods remove this constraint by recurrently propagating continuous hidden states. However, these methods pass a dense hidden vector as a whole, without an explicit mechanism for selecting and organizing the information needed by the next reasoning step. This motivates an intermediate interface that remains linguistically grounded without requiring a decoded sentence. We introduce \textbf{J-CoT}, a recurrent reasoning framework built on \emph{J-space}, a vocabulary-indexed coordinate system within the model's hidden representations. Within each cycle, the model computes in its full hidden space. At the cycle boundary, J-CoT expresses the intermediate state as vocabulary-indexed coefficients, carries these coefficients forward as a \emph{J-thought}, and maps them back into the model's hidden representation for the next cycle. J-CoT therefore requires neither a fluent intermediate rationale nor recurrence over the complete hidden state. Under matched backbone and inference settings, J-CoT-Zero matches or exceeds the strongest evaluated latent-reasoning baseline on every benchmark, while J-CoT-Train obtains the highest score across the evaluated mathematical, scientific, coding, and structured path-reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [69] [Analysing Self-Harm Representations in Language Models: a Cross-Architecture Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21988)
*Luis Espinosa-Anke,Carla Perez-Almendros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了大语言模型（LLMs）中自伤内容的表示方式，发现自伤信息在网络的最后3-7%层（93-97%深度）结晶，并发现Gemma-3-4B对自伤方向的表示与其他模型不同，更复杂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自伤内容检测是NLP中的高难度任务，且需要高精度以进行及时干预。理解LLMs如何表示自伤内容对于下游的自伤检测、LLM干预和治理至关重要。

Method: 使用两个数据集（X-Sensitive和SH-Detection）和四个模型，进行两个主要实验：(1) 在所有层上训练和评估线性探针；(2) 提取对比自伤方向并进行归一化分析。

Result: 自伤信息在网络的最后3-7%层（93-97%深度）结晶；最准确的探针不一定是线性可分性最好的；Gemma-3-4B对对比自伤方向的表示与其他模型不同，更为复杂。

Conclusion: 自伤内容的表示在LLMs深层集中出现，不同模型对该内容的表征方式存在差异，这对自伤检测和模型治理有重要启示。

Abstract: Self-harm content is particularly challenging to detect using NLP techniques, and is also a high-stakes task which requires the highest accuracy to enable timely intervention or flagging at-risk users. We therefore present an analysis of how LLMs represent such self-harm content, which has downstream applications in self-harm detection, LLM intervention and governance and policing. In this paper, we focus on two datasets and four models, and perform two main experiments: (1) We train and evaluate linear probes across all layers of each model on two self-harm datasets: X-Sensitive and SH-Detection. Across both corpora, self-harm information crystallizes in the final 3 - 7% of network layers (93 to 97% depth). (2) We extract contrastive self-harm directions and, after performing a normaliation step, we find that the most accurate probes are not necessarily the most linearly separable. In particular, we find Gemma-3-4B to represent this \textit{contrastive self-harm direction} in a slightly different, more intricate way than the other LLMs.

</details>


### [70] [DWT-Fusion: A Signal-Based Framework for Training-Free LLM-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22026)
*Mehmet Batuhan Özdaş,Murat Osmanoğlu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DWT-Fusion框架，利用离散小波变换分析token级对数概率序列的多尺度特征，结合校准加权投票融合策略，在无需训练的条件下有效提升LLM生成文本检测性能，在HC3、M4和MAGE数据集上分别达到0.9919、0.8477和0.7471的AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练检测方法依赖全局统计量，忽视了token级可预测性中蕴含的局部和多尺度变化信息，这些信息在区分人类与机器生成文本时具有潜在价值。

Method: 采用代理因果语言模型生成token级对数概率序列，通过离散小波变换进行多分辨率信号分解，从局部概率动态中提取检测信号；并设计了四种无训练投票变体（等权硬投票、等权软投票、校准加权硬投票、校准加权软投票）来融合多个小波配置。

Result: 在HC3、M4和MAGE数据集上，最佳单小波配置AUROC分别为0.9872、0.8185和0.7138；校准加权投票集成后提升至0.9919、0.8477和0.7471。

Conclusion: 基于DWT的多分辨率评分与校准引导投票融合能够为无训练LLM文本检测提供有效且可解释的信号。

Abstract: Detecting LLM-generated text remains challenging under zero-shot and training-free conditions, especially when detectors must generalize across datasets, domains, and unseen generators. While existing training-free approaches exploit language-model statistics as detection signals, they typically characterize a text through global measures that summarize overall model behavior. Consequently, potentially informative local and multiscale variations in token-level predictability may remain underutilized. Motivated by this observation, we introduce DWT-Fusion, a training-free signal-based framework for detecting LLM-generated text using discrete wavelet analysis of token-level log-probability sequences produced by a proxy causal language model. The proposed framework analyzes these sequences through wavelet-based multiresolution signal representations and derives detection signals from localized probability dynamics. We further evaluate four training-free voting variants, including equal-weight hard voting, equal-weight soft voting, calibration-weighted hard voting, and calibration-weighted soft voting, to combine multiple wavelet configurations without training a supervised meta-classifier. We evaluate the framework on HC3, M4, and MAGE using GPT-Neo-2.7B, GPT-J-6B, Falcon-7B, and LLaMA-3-8B as proxy models. The best single wavelet configurations achieve AUROC values of 0.9872, 0.8185, and 0.7138 on HC3, M4, and MAGE, respectively. With calibration-weighted voting, the best ensemble variants further improve AUROC to 0.9919, 0.8477, and 0.7471. These findings show that DWT-based multiresolution scoring and calibration-guided voting fusion provide effective and interpretable signals for training-free LLM-generated text detection.

</details>


### [71] [Enough is as good as a feast: A Comprehensive Analysis of How Reinforcement Learning Mitigates Task Conflicts in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22039)
*Zixuan Ren,Jinliang Lu,Junhong Wu,Yang Zhao,Dai Dai,Hua Wu,Haifeng Wang,Chengqing Zong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了强化学习（RL）训练的大语言模型在模型合并中的行为，发现相比传统监督微调（SFT），RL显著减少了任务冲突和合并后的性能退化。通过实验和理论分析，揭示了三个关键因素：在线策略数据控制梯度更新幅度、优化目标渐进减少冲突参数更新、正负样本联合优化引导无偏参数子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并研究主要关注合并策略本身，而不同训练范式（如SFT和RL）对合并效果的影响尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究RL训练模型在合并中的行为特性及其原因。

Method: 通过五个代表性任务进行综合评估，对比RL训练模型与SFT训练模型在合并后的性能表现。结合大量实证实验和理论分析，从梯度更新控制、优化目标特性和正负样本联合优化三个维度探究RL更适用于模型合并的内在原因。

Result: RL训练模型在合并后任务冲突显著减少，性能退化程度远低于SFT训练模型。RL的三个特性——在线策略数据控制梯度幅度、优化目标渐进减少冲突参数更新、正负样本联合优化引导无偏参数子空间——共同导致了这一优势。

Conclusion: RL训练范式天然更适合模型合并场景，为未来设计更高效的合并策略提供了理论基础，建议在模型合并任务中优先采用RL训练的模型。

Abstract: Model merging plays a crucial role in consolidating multiple specialized models into a single, unified model, especially in the era of large language models (LLMs). Recent research has primarily focused on developing strategies to enhance merging performance with the trained models, while the impact of training paradigms, such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), on the effectiveness of model merging remains underexplored. In this study, we systematically explore the merging behavior of RL-trained LLMs compared to those trained with traditional SFT. Through comprehensive evaluations across five representative tasks, we find that RL significantly reduces task conflicts and results in less performance degradation after merging, making RL-trained models particularly well-suited for this process. To unearth the reasons behind the superior suitability of RL for model merging, we conduct extensive empirical experiments and theoretical analyses. Our findings highlight three key factors: (1) On-policy training data in RL control the gradient updates in a smaller magnitude, reducing the risk of overwriting existing knowledge for other tasks in the model. (2) The RL optimization objective, which favors ``\textit{enough is as good as a feast}", progressively reduces the magnitude and the number of conflict parameter updates as the model converges. (3) Joint optimization of positive and negative examples in RL steers the model towards an unbiased task-specific parameter subspace, ensuring robust performance while further preventing parameter conflicts.

</details>


### [72] [Developing and Validating the Spanish Version of the Large Language Models Dependency Scale (LLM-D12-SP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22041)
*Tran Gia Bao,Mo El-Haj,Sameha Al-Shakhsi,Antonio Garcia-Cabot,Raian Ali,Ala Yankouskaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次验证了西班牙语版大语言模型依赖量表（LLM-D12-SP）的心理测量学特性。通过对386名西班牙语参与者（平均年龄28岁，55%为男性）的调查，确认性因素分析支持原始的两因素结构（工具性依赖和关系性依赖），量表具有良好的内部一致性（总Cronbach's α=0.89）和区分效度，并与网络成瘾、LLM可信度感知呈正相关，与认知需求无显著关联。该研究连同已有的英语和阿拉伯语验证，为LLM依赖的跨语言测量提供了支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在工作场景中的广泛使用，需要可靠且经文化验证的工具来评估对LLM的心理依赖，尤其是西班牙语人群（LLM使用快速增长但缺乏经过验证的心理测量工具）。

Method: 对386名西班牙语参与者（M=28.0岁，SD=6.1；55%男性）施测LLM-D12-SP，采用确认性因素分析验证双因素结构，并评估内部一致性、区分效度及外部效度（与网络成瘾、LLM可信度、认知需求的相关性）。

Result: 确认性因素分析支持原始的两因素结构（工具性依赖与关系性依赖）；量表总Cronbach's α=0.89，两个分量表分别为0.86和0.85；两个子维度相关但可区分；两种依赖维度均与网络成瘾和LLM可信度呈正相关，与认知需求呈弱相关或无相关。

Conclusion: 西班牙语版LLM-D12具备良好的心理测量学特性，结合已有英语和阿拉伯语验证，为量表结构提供了跨语言支持，并为组织情境下LLM使用的心理研究提供了有效工具。

Abstract: There is a growing need for reliable and culturally validated instruments to assess psychological dependency on large language models (LLMs), particularly as LLMs are increasingly used for task execution, decision-making, and communication in organizational and work-related settings. This need is especially relevant for Spanish-speaking populations, where LLM adoption is rapidly expanding, yet validated psychometric tools remain scarce. The present study reports the first validation of the Spanish version of the Large Language Model Dependency Scale (LLM-D12-SP), extending prior validations conducted in English- and Arabic-speaking samples. The LLM-D12 is a two-dimensional instrument assessing Instrumental Dependency (reliance on LLMs for performing tasks and supporting decisions) and Relationship Dependency (psychological reliance on LLMs for companionship and social interaction). A total of 386 Spanish-speaking participants (M = 28.0 years, SD = 6.1; 55% male) completed the LLM-D12-SP. Confirmatory factor analysis supported the original two-factor structure. The scale demonstrated good internal consistency (Cronbach's alpha = 0.89 total; 0.86 Instrumental; 0.85 Relationship). Discriminant validity analyses indicated that the two subscales represent related but distinct constructs. External validation showed that both dependency dimensions were positively associated with internet addiction and perceived trustworthiness of LLMs, while showing weak or no association with need for cognition. Together with prior English and Arabic validations, these findings establish cross-linguistic support for the scale's structure and provide a psychometrically sound tool for investigating psychological aspects of LLM use in organizational contexts.

</details>


### [73] [Scaling Native Multimodal Pre-Training From Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22043)
*Haoyuan Wu,Aoqi Wu,Hai Wang,Jiajia Wu,Jinxiang Ou,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了原生多模态预训练范式下视觉-语言模型的计算最优缩放规律，发现语言和多模态目标呈现不同缩放行为，其中多模态分配律对数据组成高度敏感，并推导了模型规模、token数量与数据混合的最优配置效率边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM仅依赖文本预训练，限制了其对多模态物理世界的感知能力。原生多模态预训练虽能实现深度跨模态融合，但其缩放特性尚未被系统刻画。

Method: 在固定计算预算下，研究基于transformer的视觉-语言模型的最优模型规模与token数量，拟合计算律与幂律缩放关系，并建模数据组成对分配指数的影响。

Result: 1) 最小目标损失服从可预测的计算律，计算最优的模型规模和token数量呈幂律缩放；2) 语言分配律对数据组成几乎不变，多模态分配律则高度敏感；3) 文本重混合在更大模型尺度下才实现计算高效；4) 原生多模态预训练诱导正跨模态迁移，提升纯文本空间推理和多模态上下文学习。

Conclusion: 该实证研究为可预测地缩放多模态基础模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable reasoning capabilities, their reliance on text-only pre-training restricts the perception of the multimodal physical world. Native multimodal pre-training avoids this limitation by training models from scratch on multimodal inputs, thereby achieving deep cross-modal integration and mitigating optimization asymmetries inherent to traditional late-fusion architectures. Despite these advantages, the scaling properties of this paradigm remain systematically uncharacterized. To address this gap, we investigate the optimal model size and token count for training a transformer-based vision-language model under a fixed computational budget. We demonstrate that minimal objective loss adheres to a predictable compute law, whereas compute-optimal model sizes and token counts scale as power laws. Notably, language and multimodal objectives manifest distinct scaling behaviors. The language allocation law is largely invariant to the composition of the data, indicating stable language learning regardless of the multimodal data ratio. Conversely, the multimodal allocation law is highly sensitive to this composition. Specifically, text-heavy mixtures become compute-efficient only at larger model scales, shifting the optimal resource allocation toward greater model capacity. Additionally, by modeling the influence of data composition on compute laws and allocation exponents, we derive an efficiency frontier specifying precise configurations of model size, token count, and data mixture. Downstream evaluations further reveal that native multimodal pre-training induces positive cross-modal transfer, thereby enhancing pure-text spatial reasoning and enabling robust multimodal in-context learning. In summary, this empirical research establishes the essential groundwork for predictably scaling multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [74] [Benchmarking Fine-tuning and Retrieval Strategies for a Multimodal Language Model on the NRC Reactor Operator Licensing Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22067)
*Isak Hwang,Yoon Pyo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了一个310亿参数的多模态大模型（Gemma 4 31B-IT）在美国核监管委员会反应堆操作员执照考试上的表现。通过对比八种模型-检索配置，发现采用监督微调（SFT）结合固定大小分块的检索增强生成（RAG）配置在14场考试中通过了8场（达标率57%），综合准确率达79.7%。未经过微调的配置无一通过考试。研究还发现，分块策略的选择取决于模型的训练状态，而RAFT方法在匹配搜索环境方面不如标准SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型集成到核能行业需要输出基于领域专业知识的内容，但现有模型在核工程领域的专业能力尚未经过系统评估，需要验证其是否达到操作员级别的知识应用水平。

Method: 使用美国核监管委员会（NRC）反应堆操作员执照考试中的14份通用基础考试（GFE）试题（2015-2021年，包含7份压水堆和7份沸水堆考试），以80%通过率为基准，评估了8种模型-检索配置：基线模型、监督微调（SFT）结合Gemini蒸馏的思维链推理、BM25稀疏检索的检索增强生成（RAG）、检索增强微调（RAFT），以及固定大小滑动窗口分块与结构感知分块的对比。

Result: SFT配置结合固定大小分块RAG在14场考试中通过了8场，综合准确率79.7%（置信区间跨越阈值），压水堆项目准确率80.2%。未微调的配置全部未通过。分块策略偏好随训练状态反转，RAFT在匹配搜索环境方面不如标准SFT。

Conclusion: 确定了实现操作员级能力的最佳微调与搜索方法组合：监督微调结合固定大小分块的检索增强生成是最有效的配置，而未经微调的模型无法达到核领域专业要求。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into the nuclear power industry requires outputs grounded in domain-specific knowledge. This study evaluates a 31-billion-parameter open-weight multimodal model (Gemma 4 31B-IT) on its capacity to apply nuclear knowledge by benchmarking eight model-retrieval configurations against the U.S. Nuclear Regulatory Commission (NRC) Reactor Operator licensing examination. We evaluate 14 Generic Fundamentals Examinations (GFE) from the 2015-2021 March sittings (seven pressurized and seven boiling water reactor exams) using the standard 80% human passing criterion. The base model is compared against configurations utilizing supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on Gemini-distilled chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with BM25 sparse retrieval over the U.S. Department of Energy Fundamentals Handbook, and retrieval-augmented fine-tuning (RAFT). Within the retrieval pipeline, we compare fixed-size sliding-window chunking against structure-aware chunking. The SFT configuration with fixed-size chunking RAG met the criterion on 8 of the 14 examinations, outperforming all alternatives, whereas no configuration without fine-tuning passed any. Aggregate accuracy reached 79.7%, with a confidence interval spanning the threshold, and 80.2% on PWR items specifically. Furthermore, two regularities emerged: the preferred chunking strategy reverses depending on the model's training state, and RAFT underperforms compared to standard SFT in matching search environments. These results demonstrate which combination of fine-tuning and search approaches achieves operator-level capabilities.

</details>


### [75] [FSE: Continual Learning for Named Entity Recognition by Fast-Slow Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22075)
*Yunan Zhang,Yang Fan,Heng Li,Xiangping Wu,Qingcai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FSE（Fast-Slow Experts）模型，通过共享快速专家和任务特定慢速专家，结合长度衰减负采样策略，在持续学习命名实体识别任务中实现了最先进性能，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘并促进了任务间知识共享。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习NER方法存在灾难性遗忘问题，且未能充分利用不同任务间的共享信息，导致模型在学习新实体类型时性能下降。

Method: 提出FSE模型：1）共享快速专家学习token级链接以高效过滤不合理的span候选；2）任务特定慢速专家仅对剩余候选进行span分类；3）采用长度衰减负采样策略缓解span类别不平衡问题。

Result: 在OntoNotes和FewNERD合成数据集上，FSE在持续学习NER场景中取得了最先进性能，各组件有效性得到验证，模型收敛更快，且两个专家均实现了预期功能。

Conclusion: FSE通过快慢专家分工机制有效平衡了知识保持（稳定性）与学习新任务（可塑性）之间的矛盾，为持续学习NER提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Continual Learning for Named Entity Recognition (CLNER) enable models to incrementally learn new entity types without forgetting previously acquired ones. However, existing methods suffer from catastrophic forgetting and insufficient exploitation of shared information across tasks. This paper proposes FSE, a Fast-Slow Experts enhanced span-based NER model for CLNER. The shared fast expert learns token-level links to efficiently filter out unlikely spans, while the task-specific slow expert performs span classification only on the remaining candidates. It stabilizes learning by promoting knowledge sharing across tasks and maintains plasticity by reducing learning burden at each task. A length-decay negative sampling strategy to mitigate span imbalance is also introduced. Extensive experiments on OntoNotes and FewNERD synthestic datasets demonstrate that FSE achieves state-of-the-art performance in CLNER scenarios, with effectiveness of each component, empirical evidence of faster convergence and expected functionality of both experts.

</details>


### [76] [MEUSLI: a Multilingual Projector for LLM-based ASR and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22100)
*Lorenzo Concina,Seraphina Fong,Marco Matassoni,Alessio Brutti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MEUSLI是一个开源的多语言投影器家族，将Whisper编码器与多语言LLM连接，支持28种欧洲语言的端到端ASR，并可扩展至其他任务（如语音翻译和主题识别）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级投影器系统仅支持少数语言（通常限于英语），缺乏开源的多语言方案。作者旨在填补这一空白，构建首个开源的多语言投影器家族，支持多种欧洲语言。

Method: 将Whisper编码器与开源多语言LLM通过投影器连接；使用持续学习技术使投影器可扩展到训练中未见过的语言。

Result: 在28种欧洲语言（含高资源和低资源语言）的ASR任务中表现优异；投影器可迁移至多语言语音翻译和主题识别任务，每语言仅需少量监督数据。

Conclusion: MEUSLI为多语言语音理解任务提供了坚实基础，支持可扩展和包容性的开源SpeechLLM发展。

Abstract: Lightweight projectors are an established way to connect pre-trained speech encoders with large language models (LLMs), mapping acoustic features into token-level embeddings for tasks like ASR and spoken question answering. Existing systems, however, typically only support a few languages and are often limited to English. We introduce MEUSLI, the first open-science multilingual projector family that links a Whisper encoder with open-source multilingual LLMs, enabling fully open-source end-to-end ASR in 28 European languages. MEUSLI extends prior monolingual pipelines, delivering strong results across high- and low-resource languages. Using proper continual leaning techniques, MEUSLI can be easily extended to other languages not seen in training. We further demonstrate that the MEUSLI projector can be leveraged beyond ASR, enabling multilingual speech translation and topic identification with only a few hours of task specific supervision per language. Overall, MEUSLI provides a solid foundation for multilingual speech understanding tasks, supporting scalable and inclu- sive open-source SpeechLLM

</details>


### [77] [Dynamic Commonsense Coordination for Empathetic Response Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22136)
*Zhengyu Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DCC是一个动态常识协调框架，通过三个互补模块（残差常识交互、关联引导过滤、迭代常识感知解码）提升共情对话生成的质量，在Empathetic-Dialogues基准上优于CEM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共情回复生成方法在理解和生成阶段复用固定的常识表示，缺乏跨阶段协调常识知识的能力，限制了共情推理性能。

Method: 提出DCC框架，包含三个模块：(1) SCE-AttnRes：基于残差的常识交互模块，整合上下文与情境常识表示；(2) AGCF：关联引导的常识过滤模块，降低低相关常识关系的权重；(3) ICAD：迭代常识感知解码模块，在生成过程中动态检索常识记忆。

Result: 在Empathetic-Dialogues基准上，DCC提升了情感分类准确率和回复多样性，保持可比的困惑度；LLM盲评显示回复在相关性、连贯性和信息性上更优。

Conclusion: 动态协调常识知识在不同阶段的表示能有效提升共情回复生成质量。

Abstract: Empathetic Response Generation (ERG) requires models to recognize users' emotions and generate empathetic responses. Commonsense knowledge has been shown to support such reasoning, yet existing approaches typically reuse fixed commonsense representations across understanding and generation, limiting their ability to coordinate such knowledge across different stages. We propose DCC, a Dynamic Commonsense Coordination Framework with three complementary modules: residual-based commonsense interaction (SCE-AttnRes) to integrate contextual and situational commonsense representations, Association-Guided Commonsense Filtering (AGCF) to down-weight low-relevance commonsense relations, and Iterative Commonsense-Aware Decoding (ICAD) to dynamically retrieve commonsense memories during generation. Experiments on the Empathetic-Dialogues benchmark show that DCC improves emotion classification accuracy and response diversity over the CEM baseline while maintaining comparable perplexity. An LLM-based blind evaluation further demonstrates that DCC generates responses with better relevance, coherence, and informativeness. The code and implementation details will be publicly available at https://github.com/Hanabi-Q/DCC-ERG.

</details>


### [78] [From Isolated Tasks to Structured Capabilities: A Multilayer Taxonomy for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22182)
*Shixin Fang,Jiachen Wo,Wenjuan Qin,Sihang Jiang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于人类认知科学的多层能力分类体系（14个能力域、91个子技能），将LLM评估从零散任务转向结构化能力分析，并对2023-2025年间15,934篇论文进行映射，揭示了研究注意力的集中与分布不均现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估的证据围绕具体任务而非能力组织，导致跨研究比较困难、能力覆盖不清。作者旨在建立一个基于人类认知科学的能力分类体系，将评估单元从孤立任务转向结构化能力，以支持研究组织、覆盖审计和评估解释。

Method: 构建了三层（原始层、构造层、整合层）、14个能力域和91个子技能的分类学。能力定义和组织的依据是人类认知科学而非LLM架构。对2023-2025年间ACL、AAAI、ICML、NeurIPS的31,505篇论文进行筛选，通过多模型标注、共识和仲裁对15,934篇LLM相关论文进行映射。

Result: 语言-语义能力（22.3%）、推理（21.3%）、规划与决策（13.5%）、感知（12.3%）集中了大部分研究，6个能力域出现在不足2%的论文中。语言-语义能力与推理共现最高（11.7%，lift=2.47），心理理论与社交推理的lift最高（30.84）。14个域中10个域的最频繁子技能出现在至少90%的论文中。

Conclusion: 该分类学将分析单元从孤立任务转向结构化能力，能够支持研究组织、覆盖审计、评估解释，并为诊断、训练和迁移提供可检验的假设。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) evaluation spans diverse tasks and benchmarks, yet evidence remains organized around tasks rather than the capabilities they probe. This fragmentation limits cross-study comparison, obscures capabilities tasks recruit, and makes coverage gaps difficult to identify.
  We introduce a multi-layer taxonomy of 14 capability domains and 91 subskills across Primitive, Constructed, and Integrative layers. Human cognitive science guides capability definition and organization, not LLM architecture. Layer assignments draw on developmental precedence and hypothesized functional support, while human-origin constructs are adapted to observable model behavior.
  To demonstrate operational utility, we screened 31,505 papers from ACL, AAAI, ICML, and NeurIPS between 2023 and 2025 and mapped 15,934 LLM-focused papers through multi-model annotation, consensus, and arbitration. Direct research attention concentrated on Language-Semantic Competence (3,551; 22.3%), Reasoning (3,388; 21.3%), Planning and Decision-Making (2,149; 13.5%), and Perception (1,954; 12.3%), whereas six domains appeared in fewer than 2% of papers. Within domains, the most frequent subskill had a median prevalence of 97.9% and appeared in at least 90% of papers in 10 of 14 domains. Language-Semantic Competence and Reasoning formed the highest-volume pair (n = 1,864; 11.7%; lift = 2.47), whereas Theory of Mind and Social Reasoning and Interaction showed the highest lift among pairs with at least 20 co-occurrences (n = 62; lift = 30.84).
  By shifting the unit of analysis from isolated tasks to structured capabilities, the taxonomy supports research organization, coverage audits, evaluation interpretation, and testable hypotheses for diagnosis, training, and transfer.

</details>


### [79] [Why Large Language Models and Humans Converge and Diverge in Evaluating Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22218)
*Pengzhao Lyu,Yeun Joon Kim,Hanlin Xiao,Yingyue Luna Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过三项研究、六种主流LLM，揭示了LLM在创造力评估中与人类评价趋同或分化的原因：LLM依赖更窄的评估标准，在新颖性维度与人类高度一致，但在需要社会、市场等上下文信息的维度上存在显著偏差；不同LLM的标准差异导致其识别出的创造性想法不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM被越来越多地用作创造力评估工具，但其与人类评价的一致程度存在矛盾证据。研究旨在厘清LLM与人类创造力判断何时、为何趋同或分化，填补对LLM评估标准及其下游影响的认识空白。

Method: 三项研究设计：研究1识别LLM创造力评估标准并与人类标准对比；研究2（N=1,103个想法）检验LLM评估与人类评估的相关性；研究3（N=1,195）操控上下文信息，比较LLM与人类对信息的敏感度差异。涉及六种主流LLM。

Result: 研究1：LLM依赖更窄的人类评估标准子集，与人类在新颖性维度趋同，在上下文维度（社会、市场、声誉信息）分化；各LLM有独特标准且广度差异大。研究2：LLM评估与人类评估中度相关，标准更广的LLM更能区分人类判定的创意高低。研究3：上下文信息显著影响人类评分，但对LLM评分影响甚微。

Conclusion: LLM与人类的对齐程度取决于判断所需的证据类型和各模型应用的标准：评估强调内在特质（如新颖性）时LLM接近人类，需上下文信息时则偏离；选择LLM评估者是关键决策，不同模型因不同标准会识别出不同的创造性想法。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of large language models (LLMs) as creativity evaluators, evidence of their alignment with human evaluations remains mixed, raising the question of when and why their judgments converge with or diverge from human judgments. Across three studies and six widely used LLMs, we addressed this gap by identifying the standards underlying LLM creativity evaluation and examining their downstream implications. Study 1 showed that LLMs generally relied on a narrower subset of human creativity evaluation standards. Convergence with human standards was strongest in the novelty dimension, whereas divergence was clearest in the contextual dimension, which captures social, market, and reputational information. Moreover, each LLM exhibited distinct, model-specific standards that varied substantially in breadth. These differences in evaluation standards were reflected in actual creativity judgments. Study 2 (N = 1,103 ideas) showed that LLM evaluations were moderately correlated with human evaluations, and individual LLMs with broader standards better distinguished ideas humans judged as more versus less creative. Study 3 (N = 1,195) showed that LLMs were less sensitive to contextual information: such information significantly altered human creativity ratings but left LLM ratings largely unchanged. Together, our findings help explain the mixed evidence on LLM-human alignment, showing that alignment depends on the evidence a judgment demands and the standards each model applies. LLMs may resemble humans when evaluations emphasize intrinsic qualities such as novelty, yet diverge when judgments require contextual information. Selecting an LLM evaluator is therefore a consequential decision: different models, applying different standards, recognize different ideas as creative.

</details>


### [80] [Biomedical Machine Translation for Low-Resource Arabic-Script Languages via Cross-Lingual Transfer and LoRA Adapter Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22300)
*Abdullah Alabdullah,Arash Eslamighayour,Sarp Harbalioglu,Lifeng Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对阿拉伯语系低资源语言的生物医学NMT问题，利用阿拉伯语和波斯语作为高资源枢轴语言，通过LoRA微调小规模仅解码器LLM训练领域特定枢轴适配器，评估三种转移策略。实验表明，500句监督适应可使Dari接近枢轴语言质量（CHrF++ 41.01），适配器融合在零额外成本下达到监督适应3.5 CHrF++以内。Pashto和Sorani Kurdish因与枢轴语言结构距离过大，效果不足以支持临床部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学NMT资源在阿拉伯语系低资源语言中严重匮乏，现有研究多聚焦高资源语言，缺乏针对此类低资源语言的系统性跨语言迁移研究。

Method: 使用LoRA在小规模仅解码器LLM上训练领域特定枢轴适配器，评估三种跨语言迁移策略：少样本上下文学习、最小监督适应（500句）、零数据LoRA适配器融合。

Result: 监督适应在Dari上达到CHrF++ 41.01；适配器融合在Dari上仅比监督适应低3.5 CHrF++（零成本）；Pashto和Sorani Kurdish效果不佳。

Conclusion: LoRA适配器融合对密切相关语言效果显著，但跨语言迁移效果受限于与枢轴语言的结构距离；Pashto和Sorani Kurdish尚无法用于高风险临床场景。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of healthcare-domain cross-lingual transfer to address the scarcity of biomedical NMT resources for Arabic-script languages. We use Arabic and Persian as higher-resource pivots to improve translation for \textbf{four severely low-resource} targets: Dari (Afghan Persian, a standardised variety of Persian), Pashto, Sorani Kurdish (Central Kurdish, a major standardized variety of Kurdish), and Urdu (closely related to Hindi). Using LoRA fine-tuning on small decoder-only LLMs, we train \textit{domain-specific pivot adapters} and evaluate \textbf{three transfer strategies}: few-shot in-context learning, minimal supervised adaptation, and, to the best of our knowledge, for the first time in this setting, zero-data LoRA adapter merging. Supervised adaptation with just 500 sentences achieves near pivot-language quality for Dari (CHrF++ 41.01) and meaningful gains for Urdu (28.88), while adapter merging reaches within 3.5 CHrF++ of supervised adaptation for Dari at zero additional cost. Pashto and Sorani Kurdish remain insufficient for high-stakes clinical deployment exposing the limits of cross-lingual transfer when structural distance from the pivots is too great. LoRA adapter merging works surprisingly well for closely related languages, even without target-language biomedical data.

</details>


### [81] [A Factorial Study of Synthetic Data Generation for Low-Resource Machine Translation using Grammar Books](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22376)
*Varun Ghat Ravikumar,Sina Ahmadi,Lena Jäger,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种流水线方法，利用大语言模型从语法书中提取规则、例句和词汇，生成合成平行语料用于微调机器翻译模型，而非在推理时注入语法内容。在三种类型多样的低资源语言（Kalamang、Tuatschin、Mandan）上验证，微调合成数据在75%和59%的配置中分别优于基线，最佳ChrF++提升分别为+8.8、+5.3和+3.3。通过96种配置的析因实验，识别了驱动收益的关键因素组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数濒危语言缺乏机器翻译所需的平行语料，但存在描述性语法书籍。现有工作仅在推理时将语法内容注入提示，未能充分利用语法知识进行模型微调。

Method: 提出流水线：使用大语言模型从语法书中提取语法规则、例句和词典，生成合成平行语料，用于微调机器翻译模型。在三种低资源语言上验证，并通过96种配置的析因实验（变化目标词性、检索粒度、样本量）系统分析不同因素组合的效果。

Result: 在Kalamang上75%配置优于基线，Tuatschin上59%配置优于基线；最佳ChrF++提升分别为Kalamang +8.8、Tuatschin +5.3、Mandan +3.3。通过析因实验确定了驱动收益的关键因素组合及其失效条件。

Conclusion: 静态的语言学文献可以被重新用于机器翻译微调，为严重资源不足的语言开发翻译工具提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Most endangered languages lack the parallel data required for machine translation, despite the existence of descriptive grammar books. We introduce a pipeline that uses large language models to extract grammatical rules, example sentences, and lexicons from grammar books and generate synthetic parallel corpora for fine-tuning-rather than feeding grammar content into prompts at inference time, as in prior work. Validated on three typologically diverse low-resource languages-Kalamang (Papuan), Tuatschin (Romance), and Mandan (Siouan)-we show that fine-tuning on synthetic data improves over seed-data baselines in 75% of configurations for Kalamang and 59% for Tuatschin, with best-case ChrF++ gains of +8.8, +5.3, and +3.3 respectively. Through a systematic factorial study across 96 configurations varying target part-of-speech, retrieval granularity, and sample volume, we identify which factor combinations drive gains and where they break down. Our results demonstrate that static linguistic documentation can be repurposed for machine translation fine-tuning, offering a practical path towards translation tools for severely under-resourced languages.

</details>


### [82] [grapheme-kit: Grapheme-Level Metrics and Text Processing for Multilingual NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22456)
*Izzath Nisfer,Ashini Kavindya,Ovindu Atukorala,Purushoth Velayuthan,Menan Velayuthan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 grapheme-kit，一个开源的 Python 库，它将词汇距离、相似度和评估指标从 Unicode 码点层面扩展到字素簇层面，解决了多码点表示单一字素的文字系统的评估不准确问题，并通过 OCR 案例研究证明了字素级指标在复杂文字评估中的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的词汇距离、相似度和评估指标基于 Unicode 码点操作，在单一字素由多个 Unicode 码点表示的文字系统中，会导致对错误的不准确表征。

Method: 开发了 grapheme-kit 开源 Python 库，将度量标准扩展到字素簇层面，并为泰米尔语和僧伽罗语提供了改进的字素处理能力，包括准确的字素簇识别、字素组合与分解工具。

Result: 通过 OCR 案例研究表明，字素级度量能够为复杂文字提供更忠实的评估结果。

Conclusion: 基于字素簇的度量比基于 Unicode 码点的度量更适合复杂文字系统的评估任务。

Abstract: Existing lexical distance, similarity, and evaluation metrics operate on Unicode code points, which can misrepresent errors in writing systems where a single grapheme is represented by multiple Unicode code points. We introduce grapheme-kit, an open-source Python library that extends these metrics to operate on grapheme clusters instead. The library also provides improved grapheme processing for Tamil and Sinhala, including accurate grapheme cluster identification and grapheme composition/decomposition utilities. Through an OCR case study, we demonstrate that grapheme-level metrics provide a more faithful evaluation of complex scripts.

</details>


### [83] [Skill Self-Play: Pushing the Frontier of LLM Capability with Co-Evolving Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22529)
*Siyuan Huang,Pengyu Cheng,Haotian Liu,Tao Chen,Yihao Liu,Jingwei Ni,Shijie Zhou,Ziyi Yang,Gangwei Jiang,Mengyu Zhou,Yu Cheng,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Skill Self-Play（Skill-SP）框架，通过技能作为中介协调任务多样性与验证可靠性的矛盾，实现LLM在结构化验证与开放式探索之间的协同演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化方法面临根本性困境：环境约束方法获得精确反馈但局限在狭窄领域，开放式自生成拓宽任务空间但缺乏可靠验证，误导性奖励会污染训练循环。

Method: 提出Skill-SP共演化框架，包含提议器（基于动态采样技能生成挑战性任务）、求解器（探索候选方案突破能力边界）、动态技能控制器（收集执行反馈更新扩展技能库），三者通过强化学习循环持续自博弈。

Result: 在工具使用和推理基准上，Skill-SP作为鲁棒演化引擎持续提升骨干模型性能天花板，并显著扭转初始不匹配模型的表现。

Conclusion: 技能作为中间地带有效调和了任务多样性与验证可靠性之间的张力，动态路由保持开放式任务多样性，每个技能确保特定场景下的深度可验证执行。

Abstract: LLM training is shifting from manual design and annotation to interaction-driven self-evolution. However, existing self-evolutionary methods face a fundamental dilemma between task diversity and verification reliability: environment-bound methods obtain precise feedback but confine learning to narrow domains, while open-ended self-generation broadens the task space but lacks reliable verification, allowing misleading rewards to pollute the training loop. We identify agent skills as a powerful middle ground to reconcile this tension: each skill ensures deep, verifiable execution in a specific scenario, while dynamic routing across skills maintains open-ended task variety. Leveraging this insight, we introduce Skill Self-Play (Skill-SP), a co-evolutionary framework comprising a proposer, a solver, and a dynamic skill controller. Orchestrated via a reinforcement learning loop, these components co-evolve in a continuous self-play loop: the proposer generates challenging tasks conditioned on dynamically sampled skills; the solver explores candidate solutions to push its capability boundaries; and the skill controller collects execution feedback to update and expand the skill library. This interactive co-evolution effectively bridges the gap between structured verification and open-ended exploration. Empirical evaluations on tool-use and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that Skill-SP, serving as a robust evolution engine, consistently pushes the performance ceiling of competent backbones while catalyzing striking turnarounds for initially misaligned models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/skill-self-play.

</details>

### [84] [Curly Hair Simulation using Curly Finite Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22103)
*Xinming Pei,Zhendong Wang,Lei Lan,Weiwei Xu,Yin Yang,Huamin Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于弯曲元素分解的卷发模拟方法，将每根发丝分解为杆基构型和解析定义的高频皱纹（平面波或立体螺旋），通过弯曲-能量分离方案和混合碰撞处理策略，实现了稳定、高效且视觉逼真的卷发仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷发仿真中宏观发丝变形与高频几何细节（波浪、螺旋）之间存在紧密耦合，传统方法难以同时兼顾物理真实性和计算效率，亟需一种能够分离并高效处理不同尺度变形特征的模型。

Method: (1) 将每根发丝分解为"杆基构型+解析高频皱纹"的卷曲元素，其中波状发由弯曲主导、螺旋发自扭转主导；(2) 提出曲率-能量分离方案，区分拉伸、屈曲和弯曲贡献，并通过高效能量近似提升数值鲁棒性、降低计算成本；(3) 引入混合碰撞处理策略，粗粒度碰撞代理处理基构型，解析方法处理高频细节；(4) 将引导发丝插值适配到卷曲有限元表示，在生成浓密头发时保留精细结构。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够在多种场景下实现稳定、高效且视觉逼真的卷发仿真，在保持物理真实感的同时显著降低了计算开销。

Conclusion: 提出的卷发模型通过将发丝分解为基构型与解析高频成分，配合能量分离和混合碰撞策略，有效解决了卷发仿真中变形耦合与计算效率的矛盾，为卷发的高效物理仿真提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Realistic simulation of curly hair is challenging due to the tight coupling between macroscopic strand deformation and high-frequency geometric details such as waves and helices. In this paper, we propose a curly hair model that decomposes each strand into curly elements, consisting of a rod-base configuration and an analytically defined high-frequency wrinkles represented by planar waves or volumetric helices, with deformation governed primarily by bending for wavy hair and twisting for spiral hair. A curvature-energy splitting scheme separates stretching, buckling, and bending contributions, and efficient energy approximations improve numerical robustness and reduce computational cost without degrading visual fidelity. We further introduce a hybrid collision handling strategy that combines coarse collision proxies for the base configuration with analytical treatment of high-frequency details, and adapt guide-strand interpolation to our curly finite-element representation to generate dense hair while preserving fine structure. Experiments demonstrate stable, efficient, and visually faithful simulation of curly hair across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [85] [Loom: Multi-Region Analysis of Spatial Transcriptomics with Local Neighborhoods and Global Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22505)
*Siyuan Zhao,Nafiul Nipu,Hossein Fathollahian,Olga Karginova,Hao Chen,Ameen Salahudeen,G. Elisabeta Marai*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Loom是一个空间转录组学可视化计算系统，通过新型字形设计和计算后端，支持伪时间轨迹分析、跨样本比较和局部微环境空间结构研究，经专家案例研究和可用性测试验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学数据分析面临多模态配准、伪时间模式复杂性、空间富集数据和基因表达动态等挑战，现有工具难以支持实际应用驱动的综合分析需求。

Method: 提出结合新型字形设计与计算后端的可视化计算系统Loom，集成细胞参考数据集和细胞行为时间模拟，支持局部微环境的伪时间探索、跨样本比较及时空生物学机制研究。

Result: 通过两个由组织病理学专家和肿瘤学家开发的案例研究及外部可用性研究进行验证，结果表明Loom有效支持细胞转变和时空表达动态的发现。

Conclusion: Loom系统能够有效支持空间转录组学中的伪时间轨迹分析、跨样本比较及局部微环境分析，有助于发现细胞转变和时空表达动态。

Abstract: We present Loom, a spatial transcriptomics (ST) visual computing system to support the analysis of pseudo-temporal trajectories, comparative investigation across samples and regions of interest, and the examination of spatially structured processes within local microenvironments. ST is a molecular profiling technology that measures gene expression directly within a thin tissue section while preserving its spatial organization. For practical application-driven analyses, the ST local microenvironment data needs to be integrated with cell reference datasets and temporal simulations of cell behavior. This integration is challenging due to multi-modal registration issues and the complexity of the pseudo-temporal patterns, spatial enrichment data, and gene expression dynamics. Loom leverages a novel glyph coupled with a computational backbone to facilitate the detailed pseudo-temporal exploration of local microenvironments, cross-sample comparisons, and investigation of spatiotemporal biological mechanisms. We evaluate Loom through two case studies developed with experts in tissue pathology and oncologists and through an external usability study. The results demonstrate that Loom supports effectively the discovery of cellular transitions and spatiotemporal expression dynamics.

</details>

### [86] [Three-Body Alignment: Aligning Chess Agent with Human Reasoning through Reranked Rationale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21993)
*Jaymari Chua,Chen Wang,Liming Zhu,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在象棋领域中，人类专家、引擎辅助评论员和大语言模型三种推理主体之间的"三体对齐"问题，发现它们在推理语义上存在显著差异，并通过重排序机制可以在提升人类对齐度的同时量化权衡战术性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着推理智能体日益复杂，如何使它们的推理和决策过程与人类概念模型对齐成为AI安全与可解释性的关键挑战。当建模专家知识时，理解并整合来自不同推理架构的智能体洞察对于安全可预测的部署至关重要。

Method: (1) 构建多源推理数据集，使用智能体数据工程流水线将非结构化专家评论转化为结构化数据；(2) 通过t-SNE可视化对语义嵌入空间进行实证分析；(3) 设计实验证明重排序机制可改善人类对齐，并量化其与战术性能的权衡；(4) 初步开发增强型象棋叙事数据集结构；(5) 开源象棋推理数据集。

Result: t-SNE可视化显示三种来源形成不同的聚类，证明显著的异质性，反映出对同一环境根本不同的概念化方法。重排序机制能在提升人类对齐度的同时量化与战术性能的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 不同推理架构（人类专家、引擎辅助评论员、LLM）在象棋推理上存在显著的语义分歧，重排序提供了一种可量化的对齐改善路径，为更可解释的智能体决策奠定了基础。

Abstract: As reasoning agents become increasingly complex, aligning their underlying reasoning and decision-making processes with human conceptual models is a challenge for AI security and safety. When modelling expert knowledge, understanding how to characterise and integrate insights from agents with fundamentally different reasoning architectures is necessary for safe and predictable deployment. We investigate this alignment through a \emph{three-body alignment} in chess, analysing the semantic divergence between rationales produced by human experts (Grandmasters), engine-assisted human commentators (who rationalise the outputs of efficiently updatable neural networks, or NNUEs), and Large Language Models (LLMs). Our contributions include: (1) A novel multisource rationale dataset, constructed using an agentic data engineering pipeline to transform unstructured expert commentary into structured, queryable data for alignment evaluation. (2) An empirical analysis of the semantic embedding space. Using t-SNE visualisation, we demonstrate that these sources form distinct clusters, confirming significant heterogeneity and reflecting fundamentally different conceptual approaches to the same environment. (3) An experiment demonstrating that reranking mechanisms can improve human alignment, while quantifying the explicit trade-off with tactical performance, offering a pathway for more interpretable agent decision-making. (4) The preliminary development of an enriched chess narrative dataset structure, designed to lay the groundwork for future evaluations of text rationale similarity and to address the limitations of standard dense retrieval. (5) Finally, we open-source our chess rationales dataset\footnote{Hugging Face: https://huggingface.co/datasets/jaymarichua/trichess} to support developing novel techniques that integrate diverse expert knowledge into human-aligned intelligent agents.

</details>


### [87] [Resource Allocation and Conversion along the Org Chart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22159)
*Yuan Deng,Giannis Fikioris,Chido Onyeze,Renato Paes Leme,Balasubramanian Sivan,Mihai Tiuca*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了组织层级中多种异构资源的分配问题，将其建模为市场均衡问题，证明了可行解的存在性并提出了算法，在Google真实计算资源（TPU/GPU）数据集上验证了有效性。核心创新在于两步法：先求解满足特定结构性质的“简单”（调和）实例，再通过一系列调和实例高效逼近一般实例的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的组织（企业、政府、大学）具有层级结构（如业务单元、部门、团队等），资源分配需遵循该层级约束（部分资源仅对特定子树可用），且存在多种资源类型之间的转换可能。现有方法未能有效处理这些复杂约束，因此需要一种系统化的分配机制。

Method: 将问题形式化为市场均衡问题，推导可行解的必要条件；提出两步算法：首先求解满足结构性质的“调和”实例，然后通过一系列调和实例的迭代逼近一般实例的解。

Result: 在Google真实计算资源（TPU、GPU）数据集上测试，算法有效求解了层级约束下的多资源分配问题。

Conclusion: 本文证明了层级多资源分配问题可行解的存在性，并提出了高效的两步逼近算法，为组织资源管理提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: We consider the allocation of multiple heterogeneous resources to agents who are organized according to an organizational hierarchy. In a company those correspond to business units, departments, and engineering teams. In government it corresponds to federal, state, and municipal levels as well as various agencies within each. In a university it corresponds to schools, departments, and research groups. The resources are also distributed along the same organizational tree: part of the supply is available only to certain sub-trees since it is purchased for the exclusive use of certain departments or units, and some supply is available to the entire tree. We consider the allocation with multiple types of resources where there is a possibility of converting between certain pairs of resources.
  We formulate this allocation problem as a market equilibrium problem and derive the necessary conditions to find a feasible solution. We prove that such a feasible solution always exists and provide an algorithm to compute it. Finally, we test our algorithm on a real dataset of the allocation of computing resources, such as TPUs and GPUs at Google. Our results rely on a novel two-step process, differentiating it from previous approaches. First, we show how to solve ``easy'' (harmonic) instances of our problem that satisfy certain structural properties. Then, we show how to efficiently approximate general instances by a series of easier harmonic instances, whose solutions converge efficiently to the solution of the original problem.

</details>

### [88] [Joint Load Balancing and Transmit Power Control for Energy Efficiency Maximization in the Satellite-Cell-Free Massive MIMO Uplink](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21860)
*Ngo Tran Anh Thu,Lo Hai Long,Le Duc Anh Vu,Trinh Van Chien,Lajos Hanzo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种混合卫星-无小区大规模MIMO系统，其中多颗低轨卫星与地面接入点协同为用户提供服务。在非理想信道状态信息和实际用户关联约束下，推导了上行遍历吞吐量的闭式表达式，并提出了基于改进差分进化的能效优化框架。结果表明，该混合方案在网络能效和吞吐量方面均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非地面与地面基础设施的无缝集成是下一代无线网络实现泛在连接的关键，而现有研究缺乏对混合卫星-无小区大规模MIMO系统中用户关联与功率控制的联合优化分析。

Method: 在空间相关Rician衰落信道下，采用最大比合并推导上行遍历吞吐量的闭式表达式；将能效优化问题建模为联合用户关联与功率控制的NP-hard问题，提出改进差分进化框架进行多项式时间求解。

Result: 验证了所提混合方案在频谱效率、速率公平性和能效方面的性能增益；IDE框架在大规模场景下能够提供实用的用户-卫星-AP关联指导，实现可扩展的性能提升。

Conclusion: 混合卫星-无小区Massive MIMO方案能够有效提升网络能效和吞吐量，所提出的IDE优化框架为实际部署提供了可行的关联策略参考。

Abstract: The seamless integration of non-terrestrial and terrestrial infrastructures is a key enabler for ubiquitous connectivity in next-generation (NG) wireless networks. We investigate a hybrid satellite-cell-free Massive MIMO system, where multiple low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellites jointly serve users in unison with terrestrial access points (APs) under realistic imperfect channel state information and practical user association constraints. We first derive closed-form expressions of the uplink ergodic throughput by exploiting maximum ratio combining (MRC) for transmission over spatially correlated Rician fading channels. Our analysis reveals the characteristic impact of both user-satellite and user-AP association patterns on both the spectral efficiency and rate-fairness achieved. We then formulate an energy efficiency optimization problem under joint user association and power control. Since the problems are inherently NP-hard due to the binary nature of the user-association variables, we develop an improved Differential Evolution (IDE) framework that efficiently explores the feasible solutions in polynomial time. Numerical results validate our analysis and show that the proposed hybrid scheme substantially improves energy efficiency and network throughput. For large-scale scenarios, the DE framework provides practical user-satellite-AP association guidelines, enabling scalable performance gains.

</details>


### [89] [Trade-off for Secure UAV-ISCC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21939)
*Hongjiang Lei,Jun He,Congke Jiang,Ki-Hong Park,Wenqian Shen,Liang Yang,Gaofeng Pan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究无人机辅助的集成感知、通信与计算（ISCC）系统中安全通信速率、雷达估计速率与计算能效之间的性能权衡，通过联合优化无人机3D轨迹、波束赋形、用户调度和计算频率，建立性能边界并探索三者之间的折中关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统独立架构的感知、通信与计算系统存在资源浪费和效率低下的问题，ISCC系统通过资源共享与协同设计有望突破这一局限，但如何在安全通信、感知性能和计算能效之间实现最优权衡尚不清楚。

Method: 针对无人机辅助ISCC系统，联合优化3D轨迹、波束赋形、用户调度和计算频率，分别以最大化平均保密速率、感知速率和计算能效为目标构建三个优化问题，进而通过最大化三者归一化加权和来探索性能权衡。

Result: 建立了无人机辅助ISCC系统在不同场景下的安全通信、感知与计算性能边界，揭示了三者之间的相互制约关系，为性能协调设计提供了理论依据。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化设计可以有效平衡安全通信速率、雷达估计速率与计算能效之间的权衡，为空中ISCC系统的性能协调设计奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: The integrated sensing, communication, and computing (ISCC) system overcomes the limitations of conventional standalone architectures. Through resource sharing and collaborative design, it dynamically optimizes and jointly enhances communication, sensing, and computing performance, thereby significantly improving overall system efficiency. This work investigates the performance trade-off among secure communication rate, radar estimation rate, and computational energy efficiency in an uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV)-assisted ISCC system. By jointly optimizing the UAV's three-dimensional (3D) trajectory, beamforming, user scheduling, and computational frequency, three optimization problems are formulated to maximize the average secrecy rate, sensing rate, and computational energy efficiency, respectively, thus establishing the system's performance boundaries under diverse scenarios. On this basis, the trade-off among security, sensing, and computation is further explored with the goal of maximizing the normalized weighted sum of the three performance metrics, which provides a theoretical basis for the performance-coordinated design of aerial ISCC systems.

</details>


### [90] [Binary Cyclic Codes With Simultaneously Large Minimum Distances and Dual Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22137)
*Ziling Heng,Jiantao Hu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对偶数s，构造了参数为[2^s-1, k]的二元循环码，其码率约为1/2，且最小距离d和对偶距离d^\perp同时较大。部分构造得到d·d^\perp下界接近n或2n的码，且多数码的最小距离优于已知同参数码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元循环码是重要的线性码，具有高效编解码算法。现有研究中，虽然已有大量k接近n/2且d≥√n的二元循环码，但同时具有大最小距离和大对偶距离的码族仍然很少。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对偶数s，构造了两类二元循环码：(1) 参数为[2^s-1, 2^{s-1}±1, d]的码，d·d^\perp下界接近n或2n；(2) 参数为[2^s-1, 2^{s-1}+c, d]的码，其中c在特定范围内，同样使d·d^\perp下界接近n或2n。

Result: 新构造的码在相同长度和维度下，多数具有比已知二元循环码更大的最小距离。部分码的参数经过验证，优于Code Tables上的记录。

Conclusion: 本文成功构造了一类同时具有大最小距离和大对偶距离的二元循环码族，为二元循环码的参数优化提供了新贡献。

Abstract: Binary cyclic codes are an important class of linear codes that admit highly efficient encoding and decoding algorithms. Constructing binary cyclic codes with favorable parameters has been an active research topic for several decades. In recent years, substantial progress has been made in the study of binary $[n,k,d]$ cyclic codes with $k$ close to $n/2$ and $d\geq \sqrt{n}$. Nevertheless, among the known codes of this type, only a few families simultaneously possess both large minimum distances and large dual distances. In this paper, for even $s$, we focus on new constructions of $[2^{s}-1,k]$ binary cyclic codes with rate approximately $1/2$, for which both the minimum distance $d$ and the dual distance $d^\perp$ are simultaneously large. Some of the constructions yield binary cyclic codes with parameters $[2^s-1,2^{s-1}\pm 1,d]$ such that the lower bounds of $d\cdot d^\perp$ are close to $n$ or $2n$. The other constructions yield binary cyclic codes with parameters $[2^s -1, 2^{s-1} + c, d]$ for some $ 1- \frac{5s}{2} \leq c \leq \frac{5s}{2} -1$ such that the lower bounds of $d\cdot d^\perp$ are close to $n$ or $2n$. Compared with the known binary cyclic codes of the same length and dimension, the majority of the binary cyclic codes we construct exhibit larger minimum distances. In particular, we obtain several codes with excellent parameters, which are verified against the Code Tables available at http://www.codetables.de/.

</details>


### [91] [Finite-Support Structure in i.i.d.-Constrained Capacity of Finite-Memory Poisson Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22234)
*Renzhi Yuan,Mugen Peng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有有限码间干扰的离散时间泊松信道，在峰值和平均光强度约束下，证明了在独立同分布输入类中最大化平稳互信息率的输入分布具有有限支撑集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间泊松信道是直接检测光链路的自然模型，其中同时存在多径记忆和信号相关散粒噪声。研究这类信道在约束下的容量问题具有重要的理论和实际意义。

Method: 首先对连续状态隐马尔可夫输出过程建立滤波-遗忘估计，并证明其对所有可容许的i.i.d.输入律一致成立；然后推导熵率的一阶变分，构造相应影响函数的全纯延拓；最后结合Karush-Kuhn-Tucker条件与超线性增长论证完成证明。

Result: 证明了在独立同分布输入类中，最大化平稳互信息率的每个输入分布都具有有限支撑集。

Conclusion: 有限码间干扰的离散时间泊松信道在i.i.d.输入约束下，最优输入分布具有有限支撑性质，这为实际光通信系统的输入设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Discrete-time Poisson channels with finite intersymbol interference provide a natural model for direct-detection optical links in which multipath memory and signal-dependent shot noise appear simultaneously. Under peak and average optical-intensity constraints, we study the independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.)-constrained capacity problem of such channels. We prove that every input distribution maximizing the stationary mutual information rate within the i.i.d. input class has finite support. The proof is carried out directly on the entropy rate of the continuous-state hidden Markov output process induced by the finite-memory channel. We first establish a filtering-forgetting estimate whose constants are uniform over all admissible i.i.d. input laws. We then derive the entropy-rate first variation, construct a holomorphic extension of the corresponding influence function, and combine the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker condition with a supralinear growth argument.

</details>


### [92] [Over-the-Air Interference Nulling Using Active RIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22239)
*Junzhi Wang,Jun Sun,Limin Liao,Xiangbai Liao,Yingzhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究在有源可重构智能表面（RIS）辅助的双向K用户干扰信道中实现全自由度（DoF）传输的可行性。通过高维凸几何工具，推导了实现无干扰传输所需反射单元（RE）数量的严格缩放阈值，并进一步分析了存在电路功耗时的总功率与RE数量阈值，揭示了RIS发射功率与电路功耗之间的基本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干扰从根本上限制了密集无线网络的性能，可重构智能表面（RIS）作为一种有前景的手段，有望在自由度（DoF）意义上实现无干扰传输。本文旨在探索在双向K用户干扰信道中通过有源RIS实现全DoF的可行性。

Method: 采用高维凸几何工具，将有源RIS辅助下的空中干扰置零问题建模为带有耦合非线性约束的约束随机线性系统，推导了实现全DoF传输所需反射单元（RE）数量的严格缩放阈值。进一步扩展分析至每个RE存在电路功耗的情况，在总功率预算下建立总功率与RE数量的阈值条件。

Result: 推导了有源RIS在双向K用户干扰信道中实现全DoF传输所需RE数量的严格缩放阈值；在考虑电路功耗时，给出了总功率与对应RE数量的阈值条件，揭示了RIS发射功率与电路功耗之间的权衡关系。仿真结果验证了理论分析的正确性。

Conclusion: 有源RIS能够在双向K用户干扰信道中实现全DoF传输，但需满足一定的RE数量阈值；在存在电路功耗的实际场景下，需在发射功率与电路功耗之间进行权衡，存在对应的总功率与RE数量阈值条件。

Abstract: Interference fundamentally limits the performance of dense wireless networks, and reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) have recently emerged as a promising means of enabling interference-free transmission in the Degrees-of-Freedom (DoF) sense. This paper investigates the feasibility of achieving full DoF in a two-way K-user interference channel-a canonical interference-limited setting-by employing an active RIS. Unlike its passive counterpart, an active RIS is subject to both per-element gain constraints and a total reflection-power constraint, which renders over-the-air interference nulling equivalent to solving a constrained random linear system with coupled nonlinear constraints. By leveraging tools from high-dimensional convex geometry, we derive a tight scaling threshold on the required number of reflecting elements (REs) for full-DoF transmission. We further extend the analysis to scenarios where each RE incurs circuit power consumption under a total power budget, leading to a fundamental tradeoff between RIS transmit power and circuit power. For this setting, we establish the thresholds for both the total power and the corresponding number of REs required to achieve interference-free transmission. Simulation results validate the theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [93] [Over-the-Air Interference Nulling Using Passive RIS for Two-Way K-User Interference Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22259)
*Junzhi Wang,Jun Sun,Limin Liao,Xiangbai Liao,Yingzhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在双向K用户干扰信道中，利用无源可重构智能表面（RIS）实现无干扰传输（自由度DoF）的可能性。通过高维凸几何框架，证明了干扰可以在空中被中和，并建立了所需RIS元件数量的充分必要条件，同时分析了非完美信道状态信息的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干扰是无线网络性能的根本瓶颈，RIS作为一种有前景的干扰抑制技术，本文旨在探索在干扰最严重的双向K用户干扰信道中，借助无源RIS能否实现无干扰传输（自由度意义上的）。

Method: 利用无线信道的高维随机特性，将问题置于高维凸几何框架中，利用高维空间中的浓度现象，同时处理RIS反射系数的单位模约束以及直接信道与反射信道强度差异两大挑战。

Result: 建立了实现无干扰自由度所需RIS元件数量的充分和必要条件，两者在阶的意义上一致；同时证明了在信道状态信息误差低于某个阈值时，无干扰自由度仍可实现。

Conclusion: 在双向K用户干扰信道中，借助无源RIS可以在空中中和干扰，实现无干扰传输，且所需RIS元件数量有明确的上下界；对信道估计误差具有一定的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Interference constitutes the fundamental performance bottleneck in wireless networks. Meanwhile, reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) has emerged as a promising technique for interference mitigation by directly modifying wireless channels. In this paper, we are interested in the following problem: whether \textit{interference-free} transmission (in terms of Degree-of-Freedom, DoF) can be achieved with the aid of passive RIS in the two-way K-user interference channel, which is regarded as the most severely interfered network. We show that the answer is affirmative, i.e., interference in this network can be neutralized over the air. To accomplish this goal, two prominent challenges arise: i) the unit-modulus constraint on each RIS reflecting coefficient; ii) the significant disparity between the strengths of the direct and reflective channels. To address these challenges, we exploit the high-dimensional and random nature of wireless channels. Specifically, we cast the problem within a high-dimensional convex geometric framework, which enables us to leverage the ubiquitous \textit{concentration} phenomenon in high-dimensional spaces. Based on this framework, we establish both sufficient and necessary conditions on the required number of RIS elements to achieve interference-free DoF, which turns out to \textit{coincide} in order sense. Furthermore, we characterize the impact of imperfect channel state information (CSI) on the achievable DoF and show that interference-free DoF remains achievable if the CSI error is below a certain threshold. Simulation results validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [94] [On the Maximality of Additive Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22297)
*Tim Alderson*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了加法码（additive codes）的ABS模型扩展问题。首先将线性码的Alderson-Bruen-Silverman (ABS)模型推广到加法码设置，证明一个码存在ABS模型当且仅当它等价于非退化的加法码。随后探讨了可扩展的加法码是否一定存在加法扩展的问题。对于线性码（m=1），这是Alderson和Gács的定理。本文刻画了无加法扩展的加法码特征——其关联射影平面系统是完备的，并证明对$(n,2,d)_{9/3}$-、$(n,2,d)_{4/2}$-、$(n,3,d)_{4/2}$-码答案为肯定。但与线性情形相反，一般情况答案为否定：分散线性集和另一种方法分别构造了可扩展但无加法扩展的加法码。因此对真加法码，射影系统完备性不意味着码的极大性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将线性码的ABS模型扩展到加法码，并探究可扩展加法码是否一定具有加法扩展这一核心问题。

Method: 1) 扩展ABS模型定义到加法码；2) 用射影几何中的完备平铺系统刻画无加法扩展的加法码；3) 对特定参数进行枚举或构造性证明；4) 利用分散线性集构造反例。

Result: 对$(n,2,d)_{9/3}$-、$(n,2,d)_{4/2}$-、$(n,3,d)_{4/2}$-码，可扩展加法码必有加法扩展；但对一般情形，存在反例（如$(n,2,d)_{q^2/q}$-码和$(30,2,24)_{8/2}$-码），表明答案为否定。

Conclusion: 加法码的射影系统完备性不等价于码的极大性；猜想对素数$p$，$(n,2,d)_{p^2/p}$-码的可扩展加法码总有加法扩展。

Abstract: An additive $(n,k,d)_{q^m/q}$-code is a $\mathrm{GF}(q)$-linear subspace of $\mathrm{GF}(q^m)^n$ of $\mathrm{GF}(q)$-dimension $km$ with minimum Hamming distance $d$. We first extend the Alderson--Bruen--Silverman (ABS) model of linear codes to the additive setting: a code of length $n$ with $q^{km}$ words over an alphabet of size $q^m$ admits an ABS model if and only if it is equivalent to a nondegenerate additive code. We then ask whether an additive code that admits an extension must admit an \emph{additive} extension. For linear codes ($m=1$) this is a theorem of Alderson and Gács. We characterize the additive codes admitting no additive extension as those whose associated projective system of flats is complete, and we prove that the answer to the question above is again affirmative for $(n,2,d)_{9/3}$-, $(n,2,d)_{4/2}$-, and $(n,3,d)_{4/2}$-codes. In contrast with the linear case, we show that the answer is negative in general. Scattered linear sets yield, for each square $q$, extendable additive $(n,2,d)_{q^2/q}$-codes admitting no additive extension. Further, a different method yields an extendable additive $(30,2,24)_{8/2}$-code with no additive extension. Consequently, for properly additive codes, completeness of the associated projective system does not imply maximality of the code. We conjecture that extendable $(n,2,d)_{p^2/p}$-codes, $p$ prime, always admit additive extensions.

</details>


### [95] [Robust Berrut-Approximated Coded Computing via Discrete Cosine Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22427)
*Rimpi Borah,J. Harshan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出RBACC框架，通过引入离散余弦变换码与Berrut插值的连接，实现了在存在拜占庭节点的分布式计算环境中的错误定位与纠错，并提供了有限精度算术下的近似误差上界分析与优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BACC框架虽能抵抗滞后节点，但对拜占庭节点的鲁棒性尚未被探索，存在研究空白。

Method: 在BACC基础上引入新的求值点选择，建立Berrut插值与DCT码之间的理论联系，从而在有限精度算术下实现错误定位与纠错；并构建优化问题以选择DCT码维度及编码任务分配。

Result: 推导了多种场景（仅滞后节点、存在拜占庭节点）下有限精度算术中的近似误差解析上界；实验表明RBACC能有效缓解滞后节点和拜占庭节点，重建精度优于基线方法。

Conclusion: RBACC填补了BACC在拜占庭鲁棒性方面的空白，揭示了码维度和任务分配作为新的可优化设计参数，可系统提升重建精度。

Abstract: Coded computing is a reliable and fault-tolerant paradigm for executing large-scale computational tasks over distributed worker nodes. Among existing coded computing frameworks, Berrut Approximated Coded Computing (BACC) enables distributed computation of arbitrary non-polynomial functions through rational interpolation. Although BACC provides provable approximation guarantees and resilience against straggling workers, its robustness against Byzantine workers remains largely unexplored. To fill this research gap, we propose Robust Berrut Approximated Coded Computing (RBACC), which establishes a coding-theoretic framework for BACC by enabling error localization and error correction in the presence of Byzantine workers. In particular, RBACC introduces a new choice of evaluation points that establishes a connection between Berrut interpolation and Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT) codes, thereby enabling error localization and error correction under finite-precision arithmetic. We derive analytical upper bounds on the approximation error of RBACC under multiple operating scenarios, including straggler-only systems and systems with Byzantine workers under finite-precision arithmetic. Building upon this analysis, we formulate several optimization problems for selecting the DCT code dimension and for assigning encoded evaluations to unreliable workers. We show that these are previously unexplored design parameters that can be systematically optimized to improve the reconstruction accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed RBACC framework effectively mitigates stragglers and Byzantine workers while offering improved reconstruction accuracy over the baselines.

</details>


### [96] [Microwave Linear Analog Computers (MiLACs) for Communications: Opportunities and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22509)
*Matteo Nerini,Bruno Clerckx*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出微波线性模拟计算机（MiLAC）作为一种新型MIMO架构，通过在射频域利用线性微波网络直接进行矩阵运算（如矩阵求逆和伪逆），将部分处理从数字域卸载到模拟域，从而减少射频链路数量、降低DAC/ADC分辨率要求并减少波束赋形复杂度，其矩阵求逆复杂度从O(n³)降至O(n²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来无线系统需要更大的天线阵列和更复杂的信号处理，传统数字MIMO架构难以扩展，因此需要将部分处理从数字域卸载到模拟域以提升可扩展性。

Method: 利用线性微波网络（MiLAC）在射频域直接对通信信号进行矩阵运算，通过波传播即时完成操作；尽管网络本身是线性的，但其输出信号对可调参数呈现非线性依赖，从而能够实现矩阵求逆和伪逆等超越简单线性变换的运算。

Result: MiLAC可实现矩阵求逆和伪逆，复杂度随矩阵大小呈二次方增长（而非三次方）；MiLAC辅助的MIMO架构能减少射频链路数量、放宽DAC/ADC分辨率要求、降低波束赋形复杂度。

Conclusion: MiLAC为未来大规模MIMO系统提供了一种有前景的模拟计算辅助架构，但仍面临诸多挑战，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Future wireless systems will require ever larger antenna arrays and heavier signal processing, making conventional digital multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) architectures difficult to scale. In this paper, we show that a possible solution is to offload part of the processing from the digital to the analog domain. This can be done through linear microwave networks designed to compute directly using the communication signals at radio frequency (RF). These networks, denoted as microwave linear analog computers (MiLACs), can perform useful matrix operations instantly through wave propagation. Remarkably, although MiLACs are linear, the output signals can depend nonlinearly on the tunable parameters of the network, enabling the computation of operations beyond simple linear transforms. In particular, MiLACs can realize matrix inversion and pseudo-inversion with complexity scaling quadratically with matrix size, rather than cubically, which is essential in zero-forcing beamforming. We then review how MiLAC-aided MIMO architectures can reduce the number of RF chains, relax the resolution requirements on digital-to-analog converters (DACs) and analog-to-digital converters (ADCs), and decrease the beamforming complexity. We finally discuss the main challenges related to MiLAC and promising directions for future research.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [FlowEvo: Self-Evolving Agents through the Co-Evolution of Workflows and Executable Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21596)
*Zeyu Ren,Ling Yue,Ran Li,Yishu Wang,Shengxiang Xu,Hanmo Liu,Shaowu Pan,Shimin Di*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowEvo是一个免训练框架，通过将成功的工作流轨迹编译为可复用的技能记录并存储在技能库中，使LLM Agent能在推理时积累和复用任务求解能力，无需更新模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent在推理时构建的工作流虽然能灵活解决问题，但其中发现的有用流程通常是短暂的——仅帮助解决当前任务，而无法以系统化的形式保留下来造福未来任务。

Method: FlowEvo包含三个耦合机制：(1) 工作流到技能编译——从成功轨迹中提取可复用的可执行工件；(2) 技能到工作流反馈——通过直接执行或结构化上下文注入检索已积累的技能以支持未来问题求解；(3) 技能筛选——监控下游效用并抑制导致负迁移的技能。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，FlowEvo达到82.8%成功率，比最强基线高出23.6个百分点，且每轮平均token用量不到最有效基线的一半。在HumanEval和GSM8K上也取得了最佳准确率-成本权衡。消融实验证实每个机制均对整体结果有贡献。

Conclusion: FlowEvo通过工作流→技能→工作流的反馈循环，使Agent能够在不更新模型参数的情况下，随时间积累和精炼任务求解能力，实现了更好的准确率-成本权衡。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly solve complex tasks by constructing inference-time workflows that combine reasoning, tool use, and code execution. While such workflows enable flexible problem solving, the useful procedures discovered during execution are often transient: they help solve the current task but are not retained in a form that can systematically benefit future tasks. We present FlowEvo, a training-free framework that compiles successful traces into reusable skill records. Each record pairs a callable artifact with auxiliary structured guidance, and admission applies interface, replay, and safety checks where feasible. These skill records persist in a skill bank at inference time. FlowEvo is organized around three coupled mechanisms: (1)~workflow-to-skill compilation, which extracts reusable executable artifacts from successful traces; (2)~skill-to-workflow feedback, which retrieves accumulated skills to support future problem solving through either direct execution or structured context injection; and (3)~skill curation, which monitors downstream utility and suppresses skills that cause negative transfer. Through this workflow--skill--workflow feedback loop, FlowEvo enables agents to accumulate and refine task-solving capability over time without updating model parameters. Experiments on benchmarks spanning interactive environments (ALFWorld) and code/math generation (HumanEval, GSM8K) show that FlowEvo achieves the best accuracy-cost tradeoff among the evaluated baselines under our implementation settings. On ALFWorld, FlowEvo achieves an 82.8\% success rate, 23.6 percentage points above the strongest baseline, while its average token usage per episode is less than half that of the most efficient baseline. Controlled ablations confirm that each mechanism contributes to the overall result. The code is public at https://github.com/DEFENSE-SEU/FlowEvo.

</details>


### [98] [Risk Is Not the Target: A Monotonic Framework for Evaluating Wildfire Operational Risk Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21597)
*Nicolas Caron,Christophe Guyeux,Maxime Coulmeau,Benjamin Aynes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出使用F1-score、IoU等标准机器学习指标评估 wildfire 风险系统存在根本性缺陷，提出了一种单调性评估框架，通过测量预测风险分数与观测操作负载（如火灾数量、干预时间、部署资源）之间的一致性来评估系统。在法国Alpes-Maritimes省对DFE、GRU和FARS三种方法进行实验，发现DFE尽管分类指标差但单调性最佳，GRU局部单调性好但风险分布不均，FARS暴露了上游信号的结构局限。核心结论：好的风险模型不是准确预测火灾，而是其序数标度能有效解释操作动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 wildfire 风险系统评估使用F1-score或IoU等标准机器学习指标存在根本缺陷——这些指标评估的是事件预测准确性，而非连续风险信号的操作一致性。需要一种新的评估框架来衡量风险分数与实际操作负载（火灾数量、干预时间、部署资源）之间的一致性。

Method: 提出了一种单调性评估框架，测量预测风险分数增加是否与观测操作负载增加一致。比较三种不同方法：基于专家的DFE指数、基于GRU的预测模型、以及结合预测AI与LLM推理的混合多智能体系统FARS，在法国Alpes-Maritimes省进行实验。

Result: DFE尽管分类指标差但单调性最佳；GRU模型局部单调性好但风险水平分布不均；FARS继承了上游信号的结构局限而非纠正它们。

Conclusion: 好的风险模型不在于准确预测火灾，而在于其序数标度能有效解释操作动态。这是一种范式转变。

Abstract: Evaluating wildfire risk systems using standard machine-learning metrics such as F1-score or IoU is fundamentally flawed: these metrics assess event prediction accuracy, not the operational coherence of a continuous risk signal. This work proposes a novel monotonic evaluation framework that measures whether increases in a predicted risk score consistently correspond to increases in observed operational load, such as number of fires, intervention time, and deployed resources. Moreover, we compare three structurally different approaches on the French Alpes-Maritimes department: the expert-based DFE index, GRU- based predictive models, and FARS, a hybrid multi-agent system combining predictive AI with LLM-based reasoning. Experimental results reveal that the DFE, despite poor classification metrics, exhibits the most balanced monotonic behavior across the full risk scale. GRU models achieve strong local monotonicity but fail to produce well-distributed risk levels. FARS inherits and reveals the structural limitations of upstream signals rather than correcting them. The central finding is a paradigm shift: a good risk model does not predict fires accurately, but one whose ordinal scale meaningfully explains operational dynamics, as proved in this paper. Code of the monotonic framework is available on github.

</details>


### [99] [Securing Multimodal AI through Internal Information Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21600)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Hyeonjeong Ha,Qiusi Zhan,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowGuard通过监控多模态大模型内部跨模态一致性来检测对抗攻击，利用FlowVectors量化冗余、协同和模态主导信息，在仅用良性数据训练的单类分类器上将攻击成功率从>90%降至<15%，且效用损失<3%、延迟降低6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型引入单模态系统不存在的攻击面：攻击者可将恶意意图分布到不同模态以绕过单模态安全机制。现有防御要么检查原始输入要么检查输出，忽视了内部融合过程，导致脆弱且计算开销大。

Method: 提出FlowGuard轻量级推理时防御框架，通过监控内部多模态一致性检测有害输入。基于部分信息分解推导FlowVectors，量化跨模态冗余、协同和模态特定主导性，捕获融合后的多模态预测是否与单模态语义证据对齐。使用仅良性数据训练的单类分类器进行异常检测。

Result: 在未见过的攻击上，将攻击成功率从>90%降至<15%，效用损失<3%，延迟降低高达6倍。

Conclusion: 监控跨模态一致性为多模态推理提供了高效且有效的防御方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models introduce attack surfaces absent in unimodal systems: adversaries can distribute malicious intent across modalities to evade unimodal safeguards. This motivates using cross-modal consistency as a detection signal rather than inspecting each modality in isolation. Our key observation is that benign inputs induce compatible predictive behavior from text-only and vision-only reasoning that stabilizes when fused, whereas adversarial manipulation disrupts this consistency, causing abnormal multimodal behavior. Existing defenses that examine raw inputs or outputs overlook this internal fusion process, rendering them brittle and computationally expensive. We propose FlowGuard, a lightweight inference-time framework that detects harmful inputs by monitoring internal multimodal consistency. Unlike approaches that rely on scalar confidence metrics, FlowGuard derives FlowVectors inspired by Partial Information Decomposition that quantify cross-modal redundancy, synergy, and modality-specific dominance, capturing whether fused multimodal predictions remain aligned with unimodal semantic evidence. In a one-class classification problem trained solely on benign data, FlowGuard reduces Attack Success Rates from >90% to <15% on unseen attacks, with <3% utility loss and up to a 6 times latency reduction. Our results demonstrate that monitoring cross-modal consistency offers an efficient and effective defense for multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [100] [From Frame-Level Recognition to Event-Level Confirmation: Repair Traces and Runtime Failure Analysis of Public-Space Gesture Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21601)
*M. Meng,Yansong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析景区互动信息亭项目的8条工程维修记录，提取出20个失败实例，并将其归纳为六类工作失败类别，提出了一个事件级运行时抽象来弥合手势识别与交互任务之间的差距，即“识别到交互的鸿沟”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将公共空间手势交互作为帧级识别问题评估，但实际部署系统中用户关注的是意图动作能否成为稳定的交互事件，而非单帧手部关键点的正确性，存在“识别到交互的鸿沟”。

Method: 分析来自景区互动信息亭项目的8条工程维修记录，涵盖四种手势任务，从中提取20个失败实例，归纳为六类非互斥的工作失败类别，并构建事件级运行时抽象。

Result: 识别出六类工作失败：模型输出退化、时间错配、几何尺度不稳定、坐标渲染不匹配、运行时生命周期失败、反馈同步与恢复失败。提出了一个事件级运行时抽象作为修复机制的组织形式。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提供了一个部署导向的失败分类法、一个事件确认的运行时抽象以及案例研究发现，不声称提出新的识别模型或大规模用户评估。

Abstract: Public-space gesture interaction is often evaluated as a frame-level recognition problem, but deployed systems expose a different failure boundary. In scenic kiosks, exhibition halls, and service terminals, users experience whether an intended action becomes a stable interaction event, not whether individual hand-landmark frames are correct. We call this the recognition-to-interaction gap.
  This paper analyzes 8 engineering repair records from a scenic-area interactive kiosk project, covering 4 gesture tasks: two-hand bowing, single-hand fist shaking, two-hand catching control, and knowledge-graph node hovering. From these traces, we extract 20 failure instances and organize them into six non-exclusive working failure classes: model-output degeneration, temporal mismatch, geometric-scale instability, coordinate-rendering mismatch, runtime lifecycle failure, and feedback synchronization and recovery failure.
  We further organize recurring repair mechanisms into an event-level runtime abstraction between the hand-landmark model and the interaction task. The contribution is deliberately bounded: a deployment-grounded failure taxonomy, an event-confirmation runtime abstraction, and case-study findings. We do not claim a new recognition model, large-scale user evaluation, or quantified accuracy gains.

</details>


### [101] [Transferable Latency Prediction for Fast LLM Screening on Heterogeneous Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21602)
*Xiaolong Tu,Vinod K. Mishra,Venkat R. Dasari,Anu G. Bourgeois,Haoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种运行时感知的延迟预测框架，用于在异构边缘设备上部署大语言模型。该框架将推理请求表示为硬件-运行时-模型-提示配置，分离预填充和解码阶段，通过门控预测模型自适应融合静态描述符与动态硬件遥测。在Pixel设备上评估，全预测器将总延迟R²从0.953提升至0.960，解码延迟R²从0.957提升至0.973。跨设备迁移校准可将R²从负值提升至0.94以上。实验表明延迟高度依赖于设备和运行时，轻量校准可降低分析成本并支持延迟感知的LLM部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构边缘设备上部署大语言模型时，推理延迟受模型架构、提示行为、运行时后端、硬件利用率、DVFS和热变化等多因素影响，准确预测延迟对于模型选择和部署至关重要，而现有方法难以适应这种复杂性和动态性。

Method: 将每个推理请求表示为硬件-运行时-模型-提示四元组配置；将推理分离为预填充和解码两个阶段；通过门控预测模型自适应融合静态描述符（模型、提示特征）与动态硬件遥测（CPU/GPU频率、温度、利用率等）；支持轻量校准实现跨设备迁移。

Result: Pixel 8上总延迟R²从0.953提升至0.960，解码延迟R²从0.957提升至0.973；Pixel 8 Pro上预填充延迟R²从-1.383提升至0.966；跨设备迁移（Pixel 8 Pro→Pixel 8）校准后总延迟R²从-0.974提升至0.940，解码延迟R²从-1.085提升至0.927。相同SmolLM2模型在Orange Pi 5 Pro上为8.42 tokens/s，RTX 3090上为64.38 tokens/s。

Conclusion: 运行时感知的延迟预测结合轻量校准能够显著降低分析成本，支持延迟感知的LLM在异构边缘平台上的高效部署。

Abstract: Accurate latency prediction is critical for deploying large language models (LLMs) on heterogeneous edge devices, where inference latency is affected by model architecture, prompt behavior, runtime backend, hardware utilization, dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS), and thermal variation. This paper presents a runtime-aware latency prediction framework for deployment-oriented LLM selection. The framework represents each inference request as a hardware-runtime-model-prompt configuration, separates inference into prefill and decode phases, and adaptively fuses static descriptors with dynamic hardware telemetry through a gated prediction model.
  We evaluate the framework using Pixel mobile devices and validate the profiling pipeline on Jetson Nano, Orange Pi 5 Pro, and an RTX 3090-class GPU platform. On Pixel 8, the full predictor improves total-latency R-squared from 0.953 to 0.960 and decode-latency R-squared from 0.957 to 0.973 over a static-only baseline. On Pixel 8 Pro, it improves prefill-latency R-squared from -1.383 to 0.966. For cross-device transfer, calibration improves Pixel 8 Pro to Pixel 8 total-latency R-squared from -0.974 to 0.940 and decode-latency R-squared from -1.085 to 0.927.
